營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持南亞(1303)之投資建議為買進，主要考量：(1)南亞 7
月自結稅後 EPS 0.4 元，雖不如 6 月自結稅後 EPS 2.88 元，但因
6 月有包含權益法認列收益不具可比性，而具可比性的 4、5、7 月
自結稅前淨利率分別是 9.4%、13%、15.7%，歸母淨利率為 4.7%、
9.2%、10.4%，獲利顯著持續提升，符合研究部預期。營收方面，
7+8 月營收達成率 70.6%，優於預期，公司展望 9 月營收將優於 8
月，4Q26 的營收將優於 3Q26，整體營運維持正向。(2)應用於 AI
伺服器的 M6-M8 高階大訂單目前正與客戶洽談生產配合及廠區產
線對接等細節，預計將於 4Q26 完成協商並開始有明顯進展，帶動
後續營收與利潤大幅優化，去瓶頸後的 30 萬張 CCL 產能將全數投
入高階產品生產。(3)廠商持續將擴產資源湧入中高階材料，導致
以 PCB 為主軸的玻纖布、銅箔、CCL、ABF 載板等中低階電子材料
依然緊缺，研究部預期南亞電子材料漲價態勢將延續。此外，南亞
積極推動 AI 供應鏈高階材料的開發、認證與銷售，有顯著成效，
2027 年電子相關獲利占比也將提升至 98%，電子材料占比可望達
70-80%，優於研究部原先預估的65-70%。(4)子公司方面，受惠AI
需求旺盛 ， 南電產能有限但需求持續放大 ，ABF 載板漲價效應延續 ，
2026、2027 年業績皆會有明顯提升；南亞科受惠記憶體供需缺口
再擴大，利潤急速成長。(5)近期石化產品 EG 價格大漲，利差顯著
擴大，對獲利明顯幫助，若市況持續好轉，更明顯的獲利貢獻會落
在 4Q26。研究部分別上修南亞 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 13.82
元、21.64 元(原估 12.02 元、16.94 元)，分別+2,325%YoY、
+56.6%YoY，年底淨值 69.8 元、91.4 元。研究部認為 2027 年 CSP
業者對整機成本及零組件價格控管趨嚴 ， 加上上游原料新增產能於
2H27 至1H28 陸續開出，電子材料及CCL 業者議價能力會降低，因
此將電子材料目標評價調降至 20 倍，惟考量目前股價隱含電子材
料評價僅約 12 倍，評價極低，故維持南亞買進投資建議。以2027
年電子材料:南亞科:傳產獲利占比 48:50:2，電子與傳產淨值占比
約 4：6 之比例推算，目標價調升至320 元(20xPER*2027 本業電子
材料 EPS + 5xPER*2027 南亞科貢獻稅後 EPS + 1xPBR*2027 傳產
BVPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 南亞產品線包括在石化、塑膠、纖維與電子類，分佈廣泛

圖 1、南亞 1H26 部門別營收比重 
電子材料
52.7%
化工產品
16.9%
聚酯產品
15.8%
塑膠加工
12.7%
其他
1.9%

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞是全球最大塑膠加工廠，此外，其電子級玻纖絲為全球
第二大 ， 銅箔基板 、 玻纖布為全球第三大 ， 乙二醇EG為全球第
四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚BPA為全球第五大業者。南亞產能全
球布局，主要生產基地在台灣，有57個廠， 中國有34個， 美國
有8個廠，共99個生產廠。

同時，南亞持有 26.3%的南亞科為全球第四大 DRAM廠，持有
61.4%的子公司南電為 「PCB三雄」 之一 ， 穩居全球前五大IC載
板廠，也是全球前十大PCB製造商。南亞的產品線為台塑集團
中最廣泛，分佈在石化、 纖維 、塑膠加工及電子材料等，1H26
電子材料佔比由 2025年的46.4% 提升到52.7%，化工產品由
21.8%降至16.9%，聚酯產品由16%略降至15.8%，塑膠產品由
13.9%降至12.7%， 其他1.9%。 公司生產的原材料多為集團內部
自用 ， 僅約少部分對外銷售 ， 外售若對公司更有幫助的話 ， 公
司將會提高外售比率。

聚酯產品包括聚酯薄膜、聚酯纖維(如聚酯棉、POY、DTY以及
瓶用酯粒)等 。 電子材料產品包括銅箔 、 銅箔基板(CCL)、 玻纖
絲、玻纖布、環氧樹脂(EPOXY)等；塑膠產品包括膠布、膠皮
與PU合成皮等。銷售地區廣泛，以中國、美國、台灣為主。

2. 子公司南電、南亞科漲價趨勢延續，母公司南亞間接受惠 
由於AI應用需求大增 ，ASIC需求預估在2026年大幅增加 ， 此類
產品全都用ABF載板 ，ABF載板於4Q25起漲 ，1Q26及2Q26皆維持
續漲價節奏。研究部認為接下來ABF廠議價能力轉強，南電報
價得以持續上漲，母公司南亞間接受惠。

南亞科2Q26持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM及OPM分別達到79.5%及
73.7%的水準 ， 不亞於一線大廠 ， 單季稅後EPS 14.66元 。 南亞
科預期3Q26營運及OPM將進一步提升 ， 且短缺情況至4Q26未見
變化 ， 同時預期市場在2028年前不會發生反轉 ， 母公司南亞間
接受惠。

股權變動方面，董事會之前決議處分南電 3%與南亞科1%的股
權 ， 其中 ， 南電的第一個3%已經處分完畢 ， 後來董事會又通過
再處分3%， 大部分已於2Q26底處分完畢 ， 目前沒有新的處分計
畫，兩次重大處分後仍持有南電61%股權。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

2026/3/25南亞科董事會宣布晟碟 （Sandisk） 、 鎧俠 （Kioxia）、
SK Hynix旗下的Solidigm、思科（Cisco）四家公司分三次私
募認購普通股共3.5億股 ， 股權稀釋約11.3%,研究部推算目前
南亞持有南亞科之持股比例約為26.3%。 而南亞持有南亞科的
3,099萬股權處分目前尚未執行，年底前也不一定會處分，預
估執行後持股比例將調整至25.4%。

3. 電子材料營收自1H26的52.7%提升至9月的60%以上

表1、電子材料為南亞獲利主體(營業利益佔比) 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

缺貨造成電子材料持續漲價，2025年初以來最先出現明顯供
給吃緊的玻纖 ， 近期已擴散至銅箔 、 銅箔基板等材料 ， 各材料
缺貨、漲價還會延續一段時間。中低階產品缺貨主因在於高
階化帶來的產能排擠，隨著玻纖、銅箔及基板高階產品製程
精度提升，生產速度下降，不論銅箔、玻纖、基板，要生產1
單位的高階材料，都必須放棄生產很多單位的中低階材料，
導致缺貨更嚴重，此情況將維持未來一段時間。

電子材料持續成為南亞營運轉型核心，產線維持高稼動率，
銅箔基板、玻纖布與玻纖絲產能利用率達滿載，銅箔與環氧
樹脂產能利用率在8-9成之間。8月電子材料營收占比已提升
至59.8%，較7月增加2.8pct， 正式逼近六成 。 電子材料產業到
2028、2029年仍可維持榮景。

業界有開始擴產，但擴產速度本身有限制，且擴產後也還是
會面臨材料的問題，此外，PCB層數大幅增加，將使得供給增
加，幅度仍跟不上需求的增速 。 整體而言 ， 電子材料供需偏緊
與漲價趨勢短期難以扭轉 ， 銅箔基板及高階PCB的浪潮會有望
延續。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

4. 玻纖獲利率最高，獲利貢獻CCL最大，全品項OPM持續提升 
表 2、南亞各產品別 OPM 逐季轉佳 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞產品價格漲幅因客戶依賴度而異，依客戶每個月機動調
整 。 由於中高階產品供不應求 ， 加上同業將產能轉做高階 ， 導
致一般消費性電子用的E-Glass產能吃緊 ， 這對產品報價相當
有利，報價自2025年開始每個月約調漲5-10%。

獲利率方面，現階段電子材料獲利率以玻纖紗/布排行第一、
近期CCL跟載板漲幅度大，獲利率已逐漸接近玻纖，排名升至
第二，再來是銅箔，而環氧樹脂有8成為非電子用途，因此獲
利率較銅箔低些。

獲利金額貢獻方面，雖然目前獲利率最高的是玻纖紗/布，但
CCL營收規模較大，所以獲利貢獻金額超過玻纖。

持續漲價對3Q26、4Q26利潤率是很重要因素，但除了漲價之
外 ， 產能利用率拉高 ， 固定成本不變 ， 因此訂單及出貨量增加
對利潤率也有一定貢獻， 產品組合改變也很重要， 整體來說 ，
電子材料全產品項的OPM皆持續提升當中。

電子材料產品主要包括： 
I.玻纖布 
南亞的玻纖布產能排名為全球第三，佔南亞的營收提升至 6-
7%，但獲利貢獻位居前列。年產能約5.9億米(以一般布計)，
自用比重原為40%-50%， 但為優先確保下游高階CCL供料無虞 ，
外銷比例已調降至30%-40%(自用仍以市價計價，不影響實質
利潤)，稼動率自6月起持續滿載。受全球嚴重缺料帶動，7-9
月玻纖布價格強勁上揚，預期4Q26動能不減。玻纖布/紗獲利
率為電子材料中最高，2Q26 OPM已達30%-40%以上。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

日系知名同業大廠為玻纖紗 /布領導廠商，將專注於高階市
場，而Low DK1玻纖布市場供不應求 ， 進而尋找南亞進行合作 。
此日本同業大廠提供玻纖紗，而南亞可提供Low DK1產能，製
成玻纖布後再回送日廠做後段處理。

此外，日本同業大廠也有供應Low DK2玻纖紗給南亞，織成布
後製成自家CCL基板可對外銷售，Low DK2 產品預計2026年內
會量產 ， 屬長期技術互惠合作 。 初期量不大 ， 但3Q26起日東紡
供貨量將明顯增加，足以支撐公司M8以上高階基板需求，對
於公司後續CCL發展，以及高階玻纖數量擴大會有一定幫助。

E-Glass部分 ， 南亞表示各類電子與消費型載板 、PCB層數持續
升級，即使終端消費總量持平，對E-Glass的用料需求依然持
續擴大。E-Glass自2025年底起漲勢確立，3Q26價格仍持續上
漲，公司在9月會議中表示，中國同業於年中報價約2.2美元/
米，4Q26期貨已漲至2.5美元/米 ， 公司預期2027年有機會上看
3美元/米，漲價動能依然非常強勁。南亞E-Glass採大窯爐生
產，與特殊玻纖的坩堝製程不衝突。

同業因原料緊缺或利潤考量放棄中低階訂單，客戶大量轉向
原料掌握度極高的南亞，使南亞在中階與消費型基板 (如M4
等)的稼動率維持滿載 ， 南亞不會放棄E-Glass供應 ， 未來將憑
藉原料自給優勢，承接大量同業外溢轉單，充分享受價量齊
揚帶來的超額利潤。

特殊玻纖紗使用白金坩堝設備，包含坩堝設計、冷卻拉絲等
環節有許多細微變化，會影響產品的介電常數。全球高階豐
田織布機排單已至2028-2029年 ， 南亞因為有超前部署織布機
產能 ， 提早下訂排單 ， 自4Q26起每年均有新機台如期到位加入
生產。透過台灣新港與昆山廠增設坩堝擴產，2026-2028年兩
岸累計將新增達100多個紡位。

高階石英布（Q-Glass）南亞在技術上仍需突破，初期石英紗
會先從外部供應商取得 ， 但因為公司具備生產玻纖紗的經驗 ，
目前還沒放棄，持續積極投入自主研發。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

II.銅箔 
南亞的銅箔產能排名全球第二，近期銅箔佔南亞營收已超過
10%。銅箔年產能約為11萬多噸，目前產能利用率滿載，自用
比例約50%。公司計畫在惠州擴建銅箔產線，將增加2.34萬噸
產能 ， 約擴張2成 ， 預計於2028年1月投產 。 台灣彰濱地區也有
銅箔擴產規劃，主要針對PCB應用為主。

南亞的銅箔目前銷量以HVLP1/2為主 ，HVLP3穩定量產 ， 且具備
HVLP 3.5(介於3與4之間的自有規格)及小量HVLP 4生產能力 ，
另外也有一些特定規格之超薄銅箔已進入量產，HVLP 5 則是
持續開發當中，產品線正積極朝向高階發展。

AI伺服器對超低損耗要求嚴格，高階銅箔結晶晶粒需極細，
製程具物理極限使生產速度大幅變慢，投下相同資源，高階
產出噸數遠低於傳統規格，產生嚴重產能排擠效應。

加工費方面，過去多逐季協商、具價格僵固性， 但自7、8月起
開始出現密集且連續調漲 。3Q26大宗銅箔每噸售價約1.7-1.8
萬美元(含銅價約1.4萬)，加工費約3-4千美元/噸。因結構性
生產降速與高階需求不可逆，預期加工費將具備永久性上升
動能，後續極有機會突破4-5千美元/噸。

III.銅箔基板(CCL) 
南亞的銅箔基板 （CCL） 產能排名全球第三 ， 目前CCL兩岸產能
約618萬張/月，台灣 ：中國崑山與惠州約3：7， 目前稼動率滿
載，公司表示1H26 M4 以上基板銷量逾200萬張。台灣樹林與
新港廠區既有設備的小幅改造去瓶頸進度超前，已提前於
4Q26初實現30萬張產能提升 ， 約+5%YoY， 將全數投入M7以上高
階訂單。

CCL佔南亞的營收比重已於 7月達30%左右，M系列佔其中的
2/3(約整體營收20%)，M4佔M系列的營收60%(約CCL營收40%，
約整體營收12%)，其他規格(M6至M8)則是整體營收8%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

南亞CCL具備M4至M9等級量產能力，其中，M4是出貨量最大產
品，主要供應NVIDIA用於RTX 50 系列顯卡。1H26 M6-M8處於
客戶開發與送樣階段 ， 與多位知名終端客戶與CSP客戶積極洽
談大單，預計4Q26協商將陸續完成，並將迎來明顯放量(合約
會要求客戶提供保障資金)，產品組合升級亦帶動營收、獲利
明顯提升 。M9目前則是已具量產能力並進入客戶認證測試中 ，
數據回饋理想且有極小量試賣，預計大批量產時程將落在  
1H27。

CCL自4Q25起CCL每月維持3-5%漲幅，7、8月部分客戶補漲達
10-15%，4Q26將延續逐月調漲步調 。 南亞不放棄利潤依然可觀
且客戶轉單持續湧入的M4，採 「高中低階全線掌握」 ，藉由產
品組合優化大幅推升CCL獲利率 （2Q26 OPM近20%，3Q、4Q26將
進一步追趕目前玻纖的40% OPM水準）。

儘管目前台光電在M9市佔率最高，然南亞的策略是透過開發
M10等高階產品來實現「彎道超車」，一舉超越競爭對手。南
亞的M10採用自製配方，即K2特殊玻纖布搭配HVP4銅箔，以及
自研的碳氫樹脂，不需仰賴昂貴且進口申請繁瑣的石英布方
案，在未來的成本控制與產能擴張上，將具備極佳的競爭優
勢。M10目前則是還在認證中，實測Dk/Df數據已達M10理想水
準且優於同業 ， 因測試需時1-2千小時 。 整體認證持續推進中 ，
主控權在客戶端 ， 希望1H27取得更具體的驗證成果 ， 若認證順
利通過的話，2H27將逐漸放量。

5. 預估3Q26稅後EPS 4.28元，+942.7%YoY 
受惠於AI發展帶動中高階電子材料供應緊缺、價量齊揚，且
中東戰事帶動化工、聚酯、塑膠加工等產品售價隨原料行情
飆升 ， 南亞2Q26營收合計836.5億元 ，+21.9%QoQ，+27.3%YoY，
營收達成率97%， 符合預期 。 營業利益113.6億元 ，+203.5%QoQ，
+772%YoY， 主要受惠於AI產業擴大投資 ， 相關材料需求持續成
長，使電路板等電子材料產品利潤提高。而權益法投資(台塑
化、南亞科、台朔重工、麥寮汽電、台塑烯烴(美國))共認列
獲利190.9億元(原估145億元)， 優於預期 。2Q26歸屬母公司淨
利267.5億元(原估183.2億元)， 獲利達成率146%， 優於預期 ，
稅後EPS 3.37元(原估2.31元)，+87.7%QoQ，YoY虧轉盈。

展望2H26，雖預期3Q26聚酯部門因紡織用途市況不佳 ， 營收看
季減 ， 化工部門營收看持平 ， 然而 ， 塑膠加工部門營收穩健季
增，且公司看好AI算力規模於未來幾年內仍會大幅成長，邊
緣運算將帶動終端電子材料全系列產品升級，高階材料銷售
比重逐漸提高，電子材料將帶動南亞整體營收將持續向上攀
升，預期9月營運表現將優於8月，3Q26優於2Q26，4Q26優於
3Q26。。研究部預估3Q26營收924.3億元(原估877.9億元)，
+10.5%QoQ ，+44%YoY ， 營 業 利 益144.3 億元， +27.1%QoQ ，
+1,293%YoY ，稅後淨利 339.5 億元 ( 原估 268.4 億元 ) ，
+26.9%QoQ，+943%YoY，稅後EPS 4.28元(原估3.38元)。

6. 預估 2026 、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.82 元、 21.64 元 ， 分 別
+2,325%YoY、+56.6%YoY  
展望未來，研究部認為南亞電子材料事業持續增溫，公司整
體營運將進一步轉佳。預估 2026年營收3,416.8億元(原估
3,287.9億元)，+31.5%YoY，毛利率20.4%，+12.1pctYoY。全
年營業利益478.3億元，+1,192%YoY，本業營運明顯回升，加
上權益法認列轉投資子公司獲利大增 ， 預估2026全年稅後EPS 
13.82元(原估12.02元)，+2,325%YoY。

預估2027年營收4,533億元(原估3,788億元)，+32.7%YoY，毛
利率30.9%，+10.5pctYoY。全年營業利益1,099.5億元(原估
922億元)，+129.8%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 權益法認列轉投
資子公司獲利進一步擴大 ， 預估2027全年稅後淨利1,717億元
(原估1,344億元)，+56.6%YoY，EPS 21.64元(原估16.94元)。

魏建發 合格證券投資分析人員 
Calvin.Wei@yuanta.com 
葉建伸 
jasperyeh@yuanta.com

估 3Q26 稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17% 
研華預估 3Q26 營收高於第二季，主因半導體、醫療、機器人、能源等需求
強勁，毛利率及營業利益率與第二季相當， 我們原估第三季營收季增 3%至
270 億元，年增 52%，由於 7~8 月合計營收 217 億元，達成率 80%，進度
超前，因此上修 3Q26 營收 17%至 316 億元，季增 21%，年增 78%，營收
向上，估毛利率略季增 0.2 百分點至 37.9%，稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，
年增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17%，主因營收優於預期。 
北美市場訂單強勁，為未來成長重心之一 
統計至 8 月研華整體訂單出貨比(B／B 值)為 1.47，美國市場則衝上 2.15，
公司預估到今年底仍可在 2 左右，顯示訂單需求強勁。另外，研華北美公司
到 8 月底營收年增率約 52%，上半年營收約 4.32 億美元，占集團整體營收
比重約 29%。美國是研華最大單一市場，也是今年增長最為強勁的市場之一。
由美國專案設計匯入（Design-Win）金額來看，1H26 已超過 4 億美元，高
於 去 年 全 年 水 準 ， 預 估2H26 可 接 近 甚 至 超 過 上 半 年 水 準， 今 年 美 國
Design-Win 業績應可達去年兩倍以上，美國將成未來成長重心。 
北美訂單強勁，上修 2026/27 年稅後 10%/13% 
研華北美訂單強勁，包括 半導體、醫療、機器人等業績強勁，邊緣 AI 逐漸
進入推廣及成長期，加上終端產品漲價，上修研華 2026/27 年營收 9%/12%
至 1,101/1,276 億元，成長 55%/16%，營業利益率 19.6%/20.6%，比 2025
年 16.3%明顯上升，主因規模經濟向上，費用率下滑。合計稅後 189/226 億
元，年增 78%/20%，EPS 21.75/26.01 元，比原先預期上修 10%/13%。研
華 1Q/2Q BB ratio 1.77/1.44 ，7、8 月份 BB ratio 仍達 1.64、1.47，其中北
美地區已至 2 以上，接單動能強大，預估下半年 BB ratio 維持高檔 1.4~1.5，
帶動業績保持高檔不墜，維持買進評等。

營運分析
估 3Q26 稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，年增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17%，主
因上修營收 17%
研華 1Q25/2Q25/3Q25/4Q25 B/B ratio 為 1.08/1.08/1.01/1.33，1Q26 B/B ratio 大增至 1.77，主因
半導體設備、自動化、機器人等終端客戶需求強勁，且 DDR4/SSD 等關鍵零組件供應吃緊、價格
持續攀升，使客戶積極下單至未來 18 個月的長單以確保產能與料件。 2Q26 B/B ratio 維持高檔
1.44，其中北美/歐洲/中國三大區域分別為 1.92/1.25/1.21，其中半導體、自動化設備、通路、博奕
等接單仍強勁。目前看到 7、8 月 B/B ratio 上升至 1.64、1.47，預期 2H26 B/B ratio 1.4~1.5 區間，
尤其北美地區 8 月 B/B ratio 衝上 2.15，顯示公司訂單暢旺，帶動業績可望維持高檔。

研華預估 3Q26 營收高於第二季，主因半導體、醫療、機器人、能源等需求強勁，毛利率及營業利
益率與第二季相當，我們原估第三季營收季增 3%至 270 億元，年增 52%，由於 7~8 月合計營收
217 億元，達成率 80%，進度超前，因此上修 3Q26 營收 17%至 316 億元，季增 21%，年增 78%，
營收向上，估毛利率略季增 0.2 百分點至 37.9%，營業利益 64.2 億元，季增 25%，年增 137%。
業外受惠利息收入，投資收益、股息收入等挹注，獲利 5.7 億元。稅後 55.7 億元，季增 23%，年
增 101%，EPS 6.41 元，上修 17%，主因營收優於預期。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第3 頁，共 23頁  
圖 2：研華 2Q26 B/B ratio 1.44，客戶下單積極

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
北美市場訂單強勁 
統計至 8 月研華整體訂單出貨比(B／B 值)為 1.47，美國市場則衝上 2.15，公司預估到今年底仍可
在 2 左右，顯示訂單需求強勁。另外，研華北美公司到 8 月底營收年增率約 52%，上半年營收約
4.32 億美元，占集團整體營收比重約 29%。美國是研華最大單一市場，也是今年增長最為強勁的
市場之一。由美國專案設計匯入（Design-Win）金額來看，2025 年為 4 億美元，2026 年上半年已
超過去年全年成績，今年下半年可能接近甚至超過上半年水準，今年美國 Design-Win 業績應可達
去年兩倍以上，展現美國將成未來成長重心。北美市場 醫療、半導體及機器人成長最快，醫療為目
前最大應用，其中美國前 30 大醫療製造商，經過長期耕耘，研華已獲取 28 家，成績突出。 
半導體領域則因資料中心建置加快，以及 近年相關廠商積極擴產，強勢帶動研華北美業績。 針對水
冷散熱，研華未製造水冷硬體，而是提供 Edge Server 、HMI 與 I/O 設備給水冷廠商進行整合與液
漏偵測。另外公司還切入涵蓋電力、能源管理與 OT 維運，使半導體營運加快。在快速增長下，預
計研華北美半導體領域比重逐漸逼近醫療業務。 
機器人方面，業務範圍包括：機器手臂、 AMR(自主移動機器人)、協作型、人型機器人 等，目前機
器人部分營收最大為 AMR、協作型，現有增加無人機貢獻營收（如智慧農業）。人形機器人現在
都是 POC，主要來自中國、美國，韓國 ，大部分為新創公司，主要向研華買硬體，之後做成演算
法賣給大公司。機器人相關產業案子由去年的 130 幾個到現在 150 幾個，快速成長中。 
最後公司將戰略核心定調於深耕 AI 第五層的工業應用層，透過推出 IWS 一體機切入製造、能源與
醫療等行業，大幅降低企業導入邊緣 AI 的門檻，帶動整體基本面與長線發展穩健向上。IWS 有點
像目前 GB 10，只是等級更高，有望為研華創造新業績。

受惠規模經濟上揚，營業利益率上升
研華高達 70%~75%業績來自零售與自有品牌(Channel Sales) ，公司保有高度訂價主導權，但零組
件漲價太大時，仍無法全部轉嫁出去，即使順利漲價，時間仍會略微落後；另外 25%的 ODM 業務
仍需面對較大價格競爭，漲價壓力較大。
上半年不僅記憶體大漲， CPU、PCB 等原物料亦加入漲價行列，雖然研華將終端產品陸續漲價反
應，且有遲滯風險。研華 2Q26 毛利率季減 1.5 百分點至 37.7%，低於預期，但 2Q26 營收季增
28%，年增 47%，受惠規模經濟上揚，費用率季減 2.8 百分點，營業利益率 19.6%，季增 1.3 百分
點，優於預期。在原物料維持高檔情況下，未來幾季毛利率將在 37~38%。2027 年原物料價格下
滑，毛利率可望上揚，因此估 2026/27 年毛利率 38.1%/38.6%，比 2025 年 39.8%略降，但營業利
益率 19.6%/20.6%，則比 2025 年 16.3%明顯上升，主因規模經濟向上。

邊緣 AI 占比持續上揚，有助維持長期毛利率
AI 訓練經過三年後，2025 年企業端及消費端 AI 推論需求上揚，加上 DeepSeek 等低價、開源 AI
模型推動，有助邊緣 AI 銷售提升。研調機構預估全球邊緣 AI 2025 -2032 年產值 CAGR 高達
23.8%。研華邊緣 AI 目前主攻六大產業：半導體、醫療、自動化、能源、機器人、零售。未來邊
緣 AI 將由選配變成標配及 AI PC 滲透率提高，推展速度增快。邊緣 AI 2023/24/25 年貢獻營收
4%/9%/18%，1H26 上升至 22.5%，研華估 2026 年底目標 30%，全年平均 25%，成長加快。公
司預期邊緣 AI 軟硬體結合策略，比公司平均毛利率高 2~3%，有助抵消上游原物料上漲負面因素。

現金股利增加
公司宣佈自 2026 年起取消每三年配發股票股利的慣例，改採全現金配息；經常性配息率由 70-
75%提升至 70-80%；並於未來三年(2026~2028 年)從資本公積每年加發特別股利 NT$2 元，2025
年合計配息 NT$11.2 元，配發率達 91%。預期 2026/27 年在資本公積每年加發特別股利 NT$2 元
下，配發率仍可達 85%左右。
北美訂單強勁，上修 2026/27 年稅後 10%/13%
研華北美訂單強勁，包括 半導體、醫療、機器人等業績強勁，邊緣 AI 逐漸進入推廣及成長期，加
上終端產品漲價，上修研華 2026/27 年營收 9%/12%至 1,101/1,276 億元，成長 55%/16%，上游
原物料大漲，雖公司終端產品轉嫁因應，但難以全移轉且有遲滯風險，估 2026/27 年毛利率
38.1%/38.6%，比 2025 年 39.8%略降，但營業利益率 19.6%/20.6%，則比 2025 年 16.3%明顯上

升，主因規模經濟向上，費用率下滑。合計稅後 189/226 億元，年增 78%/20%，EPS 21.75/26.01
元，比原先預期上修 10%/13%，主因上調營收。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第7 頁，共 23頁
產品結構分析
產品線含括各應用領域
研華為全球工業電腦 (IPC)與物聯網龍頭，其產品與核心業務主要劃分為以下五大事業群結構分析：
1)物聯網自動化事業群 (IoT Automation Sector)
• 核心定位：聚焦於「工業 4.0」與「智慧製造」的數據採集與實體控制層。1H26 營收占比 7%，
產品毛利率高達 65~70%。
• 產品結構：包含遠端遙控 I/O 模組(如經典的 ADAM 系列)、工業分布式資料擷取卡(DAQ)、工業
通訊網閘(Gateway)與自動化控制器(UNO 系列)。
• 市場趨勢： 結合研華自主開發的 WebAccess、EdgeLink 等工業軟體平台，提供工廠、能源與環
控系統從端點到雲端的完整數據串接。 受 惠 全 球 自 動 化 需 求 提 升 ， 估2026/27 年營收年增
49%/20%。
2)智能系統事業群 (Intelligent Systems Sector)

• 核心定位： 提供高效能、高擴充性的運算平台，作為大型基礎設施或邊緣 AI 應用的高階核心大
腦。1H26 營收占比 37%，產品毛利率 40~45%。
• 產品結構： 機架式工業電腦主機、智慧型機箱、網路安全通訊伺服器、影像應用電腦系統，以及
近年爆發的自主移動機器人(AMR)與無人載具(AS&R)控制系統。
• 市場趨勢： 隨著邊緣 AI 與實體 AI(Physical AI )需求大爆發，該事業群在北美半導體設備、網安、
醫療大單的專案導入帶動下，成為近年增速最為強勁的營收引擎 ，估 2026/27 年營收年增
63%/12%。
3)嵌入式事業群 (Embedded IoT Group / Embedded Sector)
• 核心定位：研華營收占比最大的核心支柱(1H26 占比約 35%)，全球市佔第一的工業級嵌入式板卡
供應商，產品毛利率可達 30~35%。
• 產 品 結 構 ： 包 含 工 業 級 主 機 板(Mini-ITX 、 Micro ATX ) 、 嵌 入 式 電 腦 模 組(COM-Express 、

SMARC)、工控儲存與記憶體解決方案、工業顯示器系統。
• 市場趨勢：近年主力朝向  Edge AI (邊緣人工智慧 )與 NVIDIA、Intel 等巨頭合作，推出搭載
GPU/NPU 的邊緣運算加速模組。 近期受惠醫療、半導體、工廠自動化、能源等產業出貨動能強勁，
估 2026/27 年營收年增 52%/17%。
4)智能服務事業群 (Intelligent Service Sector)
• 核心定位： 擺脫傳統工廠製造框架，將工業電腦技術延伸至「智慧生活與服務業應用」的垂直場
域。1H26 營收占比 6%，產品毛利率 35~40%。
• 產品結構： 數位電子看板、智慧零售 POS/POI 終端、車載行動電腦與物流車隊管理系統、高階
醫療用電腦與數位手術室一體化系統。
• 市場趨勢： 主要針對智能醫療、智慧零售、智慧物流等特定行業（Vertical Markets）提供軟硬整
合解決方案。2026 年因通膨及戰爭影響，零售出貨疲弱，估營收年減 7%，2027 年因基期變低，
實體 AI 需求逐漸上揚，估營收年增 22%。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第8 頁，共 23頁  
5)Advantech Service+ / Others 
• 核心定位： 負責全球後勤、DMS(設計製造服務)、全球在地化組裝與客製化加值服務。 1H26 營
收占比 15%，產品毛利率 20~25%。 
• 產品與服務結構： 提供結合硬體的 BIOS 客製化、作業系統(OS)整合；並包含營運代管服務、客
戶售後全球運籌物流(全球 4 大運籌與 21 個在地服務中心)。 
• 市場趨勢： 在「地緣政治與供應鏈重組」浪潮下，此項目透過在美、歐、日等在地化組裝與透明
化 BABA/非中國零件物料分析，協助提高研華設計專案（Design-Win）的爭取能力。北美 Design-
Win 大增，加上地緣政治與供應鏈重組浪潮下，估 2026/27 年營收年增 105%/18%。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第9 頁，共 23頁  
同業比較分析  
研華工業電腦主機板市占率領先同業 
研華為全球工業電腦龍頭，產品涵蓋工業主機板、嵌入式系統、工業自動化及邊緣運算等領域，並
以自有品牌及全球通路布局為主要競爭優勢。公司約 70~75% 業績來自零售與自有品牌（Channel 
Sales），具高度訂價主導權，且全球銷售與服務據點完整，有利於掌握不同產業客戶需求及維持
長期客戶關係。 
與主要同業相比，研華產品布局較為完整，2025 年營收以 Embedded/iSystems 為主，占比分別為 
36%/34%，產品由工業主機板、嵌入式模組延伸至完整工業系統、自動化及垂直應用解決方案 ；
工業電腦主機板市占率達 12.4%，領先全球。排名第二為 Kontron(已被樺漢併入)，主攻歐洲市場，
除工業電腦硬體外，亦涵蓋軟體、交通及航太國防等高附加價值解決方案。東擎產品組合相對集中
於工業主機板、嵌入式系統及  Edge AIoT 平台。研華憑藉較完整產品組合、自有品牌及全球通路
布局， 2025 年營收  708.8 億元，規模明顯高於主要同業，營業利益率及淨利率分別達  
16.3%/14.9%，展現規模經濟及營運效率優勢。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第10 頁，共 23頁
客戶分析
研華終端應用客戶分散
研華作為全球工業電腦 (IPC) 與邊緣 AI (Edge AI) 的龍頭大廠，核心戰略從早期的「硬體元件供應」
全面轉型為「硬軟整合平台（如  IWS 平台）與邊緣運算方案提供者」。以下針對研華指定的七大
終端應用客戶領域進行分析：
1)自動化製造(智慧工廠)：占營收比重約 30~35%
• 市場痛點與需求：傳統產線面臨缺工、設備老化、製程盲點，以及跨國工廠數據無法即時量化的
瓶頸。
• 研華解決方案：提供工業級主機板、無風扇工業電腦、以及結合視覺檢測的邊緣  AI 智能系統。
• 客戶應用效益：協助客戶落實預測性維護 (Predictive Maintenance) 與 AI 視覺瑕疵檢測，讓產線
稼動率與良率全面被數據化提升，並能將成功案例快速複製到全球工廠。
2)醫療(智慧醫療)：占營收比重約 15~20%
• 市場痛點與需求：醫療器材（如手術機器人、高階影像、導航系統）要求高精準度、低延遲、且

必須通過各國極其嚴格的醫療級法規認證。
• 研華解決方案：開發醫療專用電腦 (Medical PC)、手術室整合控制核心以及邊緣 AI 醫療影像板卡。
• 客戶應用效益：在高階智慧醫療器械的滲透率極高，以北美市場為例，前  30 大醫療設備製造商
中就有 28 家是研華客戶，穩居核心供應商地位。
3)能源(智慧能源與環境)：占營收比重 10~15%
• 市場痛點與需求：各國企業與政府面臨迫切的  ESG 減碳規範 、高電價壓力，以及分散式電網
（太陽能、風力、儲能）的調配難題。
• 研華解決方案：推出智慧能源管理系統 (iEMS)與環境監控資料採集器。
• 客戶應用效益：幫助客戶即時監控碳足跡與用電能耗，優化儲能系統配置，達到永續低碳轉型的
法規要求。
4)智慧城市(智慧零售與基礎建設)：占營收比重 10~12%
• 市場痛點與需求：現代都市需要更高效的公共基礎設施、無人化自助服務、以及大量公共數據的
邊緣端處理能力。
• 研華解決方案：智慧 POS 系統、自助服務機 (Kiosk/UTK)、一體式電腦 (UTC) 以及影像 AI 運算

平台。
• 客戶應用效益：大幅減少零售門市與公共場所的人力成本，提升無人化交易體驗與城市基建的運
作效率。
5)資安(邊緣安全與信賴供應鏈)：占營收比重 10~12%
• 市場痛點與需求：當大量設備連接至邊緣端 (Edge)，網路攻擊風險大增；此外，美中地緣政治使
得敏感產業（如半導體、國防）對「非中國零件 (Non-China Component)」要求極高。
• 研華解決方案：研華從硬體底層植入資安晶片，並提供最完整、透明的物料清單  (BOM) 分析及替
代方案。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第11 頁，共 23頁  
• 客戶應用效益：透過符合美國 BABA (Build America, Buy America) 規範的在地生產鏈，確保高機
密性客戶（如半導體前/後段製程設備商）免於供應鏈中斷與資安漏洞的風險。 
6)交通 (智慧交通)：占營收比重 10~12% 
• 市場痛點與需求：鐵路、捷運及大型車隊需要適應極端震動與溫差環境的系統，以進行即時票務
處理、車隊監控與安全防護。 
• 研華解決方案：部署於捷運讀卡機、自動售票機、車載硬體，並整合多攝影機系統（ Multi-
camera Systems）與車載邊緣運算。 
• 客戶應用效益：提供大眾運輸系統高度穩定的核心硬體，實現大數據流量的即時運算與精準車隊
車況調度。 
7)物流 (智慧物流)：占營收比重 8~10% 
• 市場痛點與需求：電商爆發帶來倉儲空間與 驗貨人力的雙重吃緊，冷鏈與貴重資產在運輸途中極
易損壞失聯。 
• 研華解決方案：工業級機器人控制器、自動化倉儲系統，以及最新推出的太陽能戶外資產追蹤方
案。 
• 客戶應用效益：為無人搬運車 (AGV) 與自主移動機器人 (AMR) 提供大腦（如結合 NVIDIA/Intel/
高通平台的運算單元），有效優化倉儲調度、追蹤跨國資產狀態。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第12 頁，共 23頁  
財務分析  
資產負債表健全，存貨上升具實質需求支撐 
資產負債表部分，研華流動比、速動比 1Q26、2Q26 出現下滑，落在 170%/100%上下，仍屬可接
受水平，負債比率落在 26%~42%健康區間。營運現金流量穩定維持正數，顯見公司資金控管得當。
另外，營運現金流近年維持在 80~107 億之間，預期 2027 年將大幅提升至 201 億元，資本支出於
2025 年達 28.4 億元，預期 2026、2027 年略減少至 21.6/19.4 億元。ROE ( 股東權益報酬率)研華
維持在 17~32%區間，屬於健康水準。 
研華 4Q25~2Q26 存貨週轉天數由 95.7 天提升至 103.9 天，應收帳款天數由 53.2 天降至 51.1 天，
應付帳款天數則由 62.4 天延長至 70.4 天，收款改善及付款天數延長部分抵銷備貨的資金壓力。同
期存貨金額由 118.3 億元增至 209.1 億元，2Q26 年增 92.3%，高於單季營收約 47%的增幅。存貨
增加主要受零組件漲價及業務成長備貨影響，由於公司銷售佳，客戶訂單供不應求，目前無影響。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第13 頁，共 23頁  
產業概況  
工業電腦未來 CAGR 6%，為下游成長穩定的產業 
工業電腦在 2000 年後網路發達，亦造成工業電腦用途日漸擴大，由工廠發展至生活端。過去常受
全球製造業景氣影響，後因應用面變廣，成長性加快。 
工業電腦近年成長率在 5~10%，是下游成長穩定的產業。根據研調機構統計及預估，工業電腦
2023 年產值約為 45 億美元，可望增加至 2037 年的 96 億美元，CAGR 6% 比一般 PC 低個位數成
長為佳，主因公司對工業物聯網、製造業數位化、邊緣 AI 建置…等因素推動，其中半導體、製造
業、汽車、物流、零售…等均為重要成長來源。 
圖 17：工業電腦 2023-2037 年產值 CAGR 6%

資料來源：Markets and Markets、元大投顧整理 
工業 4.0 帶動物聯網需求大增 
從 18 世紀末經歷多次工業生產變革，20 世紀末進入 21 世紀，工業製造由 3.0 發展至 4.0，其中最
大改變就是組裝、加工、搬運 …等由原先自動化機台 (工業 3.0)，加入智能回饋，透過物聯網收集
巨量資料，傳輸至雲端運算，統計分析後，即時改善生產製程。隨時因應機台老舊、材料或周遭環
境變化，自動調整量產系統。 
第四次工業革命和過去相比，網路無所不在，移動性提高；感測器體積變得更小、性能更強大、成
本也更低。與此同時，人工智慧和機器學習也開始嶄露鋒芒，相關應用不斷增加。

### Page 14

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第14 頁，共 23頁  
圖 18：歷次工業變革

資料來源：元大投顧整理 
預估人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)2025~2034 年 CAGR 達 22.16% 
物聯網基礎架構分為五層：物件、通訊與網路、運算與儲存、應用與服務、分析與統計。台灣工業
電腦公司較專注在前端物件 (感測元件)以及中端的運算與儲存二項業務。尤其前端的感測元件為台
灣強項，諸如：POS 機、ATM、機器人手臂、安控攝影機…等。近年受惠人工智慧(AI)導入，成長
加快。 
全球人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)未來受惠於 Data Center、AI Server 建置、企業數位轉型…等因素穩定
成長，研調機構預估 AIoT 2025~2034 年 CAGR 22.16%，為未來工業電腦成長重心。人工智慧物
聯網(AIoT)在全球使用廣泛，目前工業電腦產業使用方面已經陸續切入智慧家庭、車聯網、自動化
設備、機器人…等領域。

獲利調整與股票評價

9%/12%至 1,101/1,276 億元，成長 55%/16%，上游原物料大漲，雖公司終端產品轉嫁因應，但難

北美訂單及邊緣 AI 需求強勁，維持買進，目標價 870 元，潛在上漲空間 19.8%
研華為國內工業電腦龍頭，亦為全球第一大工業電腦自有品牌公司，與其同樣從事工業電腦的國內
同業主要為凌華、飛捷、振樺、廣積等。以 2027 年預估 EPS 來看，研華本益比 27.9 倍，比國內

同業 9.6 倍高，主因研華為工業電腦龍頭，法人佈局首選，享有較高本益比。
研華 1Q/2Q BB ratio 1.77/1.44 ，7、8 月份 BB ratio 達 1.64、1.47，接單動能強大，顯示工業電腦
景氣處於上升階段，預估下半年 BB ratio 維持高檔 1.4~1.5。邊緣 AI 需求上揚，應用面：半導體、
醫療、自動化測試設備、機器人等在各區域業績強勁，帶動下半年業績高檔不墜。 維持先前目標本
益比 35 倍，以 2H26~1H27EPS 24.82 元計算，目標價 870 元，潛在上漲空間 19.8%。

公司簡介  
研華(2395 TT) 為全球第一大工業電腦嵌入式板卡製造商，全球市占率約 27%。主要業務為各項工業電腦解決方案，如：零
售、交通、國防、基建、醫療、綠能 …等。產品組合方面 (2025)，iAutomation 7% 、iSystems 34% 、Embedded 36% 、
iService 11% 、AS+ 12% ；銷售區域(2025)北美 31%，歐洲 16%，中國 21%，北亞 9%，台灣 7%，其他亞洲及新興市場
10%，其他地區 6%。主要生產地區包括：大陸昆山廠、台灣林口廠及日本北九州廠。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026 年 10 月 1 日 Advantech 第20 頁，共 23頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 研華整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先的
位置，在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名也領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 研華的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略優於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含人力資源、數據隱私與網路安全、商業道德等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：研華針對重要 ESG 議題的整體管理能力很強，公司已建立董事會對 ESG 議題的監
督機制，其 ESG 報告部分符合最佳實務標準。公司有完善的人才資本發展計畫，包括人才招募、培育和留任的相關措
施。然而，研華在商業道德以及賄賂和貪腐風險管理上的計畫較弱。此外，管理系統已通過 ISO 27001 認證。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Taiwan ODM/Brands: 3-month preview: Aug +9% MoM on avg; new AI 
server racks and smartphone models to drive growth in coming months
30 September 2026 | 1:20AM HKT
We preview 3-month revenues for 10 companies under coverage in the AI server/PC 
supply chain, and update our estimates for Wistron and Inventec. Across the supply 
chain, we are positive on: (1) Apple’s supply chain on the back of a ramp up of new 
models with form factor changes (i.e. foldable phones in 2026, and the iPhone 20 in 
2027) to support end demand; (2) AI server components to ride on specification 
upgrades; and (3) ASIC AI servers’ rising penetration rate. Buy: Hon Hai, Wiwynn, 
Wistron, AVC; Neutral: Gigabyte, Quanta, Inventec, Compal, ASUS; Sell: Pegatron.  
On a MoM basis, we expect the average revenue growth of the 10 companies to 
come in at +7%/ +2%/ +4% in Sep/ Oct / Nov 2026 (Exhibit 1). We expect 
sequential revenue growth in coming months, driven by the next-generation 
rack-level AI server and new flagship smartphone models shipments ramp up. 
On a YoY basis, we expect the average revenue growth of the 10 companies to 
come in at +49%/ +57%/ +52% in Sep/ Oct / Nov 2026 (Exhibit 2), driven by the 
continuous ramp-up of AI servers, along with a PC product mix upgrade, new 
smartphone models, and high memory costs driving up the selling price.

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 3Q / 4Q26E; 3-month 
revenues preview

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +14% / +23% QoQ, driven by (1) AI data 
center: The next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp up, with higher ASP 
and early delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first one to deliver the latest model, 
around 2-3 months ahead of peers, driving its Sep and onward revenue growth. 
Followed by GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks would fuel 
the growth in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27, and (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlight in September (report link), we 
reduce global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m to 1.1bn / 1.2bn/ 1.2bn 
in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we remain 
positive on global leading brands to outperform the market given the strong brand 
awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger consumption power. We 
remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium models to continue gain 
market share, positive to Hon Hai. Maintain Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 3Q26E revenues to be +40% YoY/ 
+14% QoQ to NT$2.9 trillion. Both Jul and Aug delivered strong revenues YoY growth 
(+54%/ 52% YoY), and we expect to see both YoY and MoM positive growth in Sep to 
Dec, driven by new AI server racks, CPO switch, and latest smartphone models and 
foldable phones. We keep 2026E - 2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged.

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): New AI server rack ramp up to support 2H26 growth; PC slowdown; 
Neutral

With a high base in 1H26, driven by PC consumption pull-in and AI server rack 
expansion, we expect flat QoQ in 3Q26 / 4Q26 at 2% / 2%, reflecting slowdown in PC 
shipment and AI server racks facing model transitions. The company’s PC shipment 
declined by 30% / 37% YoY in Jul / Aug, reflecting the impact of PC consumption pull-in 
in 1H26 and slow PC shipment under high memory costs. We expect revenues to 
sequentially increase in Oct to Dec and further accelerate to 54% MoM in Dec, reflecting 
the ramp up of next generation AI server racks, which carry higher ASP . With strong AI 
spending driving not only AI servers (GPU and ASIC) but also general servers, along with 
customer requirement on capacity diversification, we estimate Quanta’s capex to stay at 
a high level of NT$44bn / NT$32bn in 2026 / 27E (vs. NT$15bn in 2025). In Sep, Quanta 
announced factory rental in USA at 199k square feet (US$368k per month for 126

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta

correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.

recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks, (2) its major 
ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate, (3) its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the 
lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net 
income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion; Aug strong beat; Buy

announced a 2H26 capex budget of US$942m, implying 2026 capex could be up to 
NT$40bn (US$1.3bn). On Sep 16, the company announced equipment procurement of 
US$16m (NT$512m), reflecting the strong AI demand and the company’s commitments 
on capacity expansion. We estimate the company’s capex at NT$44bn in 2026E, and to 
stay at a similar high level through 2028E, supporting its production sites diversification 
across the US, Mexico, and Malaysia, AI servers expansion from ASIC to GPU-powered, 
and products expansion across different ASICs and GPUs, along with further customers 
penetration.

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): Aug strong beat on rack-level AI server; Buy with TP raised to NT$295

Wistron’s Aug revenue was up 166% YoY to NT$460bn, or 48% higher than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, offsetting the impact of 
AI server model transition and sluggish PC end demand. Aug PC shipment was down 
13% MoM to 1.4m units, mainly on the 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising memory 
cost. We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up across GPU, CPU, and LPU. The 
company plans to invest US$209m to expand its AI server capacity in US and Vietnam 
(link), which would further support its revenue growth in coming years, in our view. 
Maintain Buy.

Earnings revision: We factor in Wistron’s Aug revenue and revise up our 2026-28E net 
income by 2% / 3% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 
11% / 7% / 6%, mainly on higher rack-level AI server shipments and ASP , considering the 
strong end demand and rising memory cost. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised down 
mainly on higher contribution from rack-level AI server, which usually carries lower GM 
than other product lines. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are largely unchanged.

### Page 15

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$61.0. Our target 
price is based on 15.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.  
Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix upgrade

3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, mainly 
driven by AI server racks ramp up with higher ASP and major smartphone brand 
customer’s new model launches (report link). Overall, we project Pegatron’s 3Q/4Q26E 
revenues to grow +18%/+25% YoY, driven by: (1) new model launches from a major 
smartphone customer supporting September and 4Q26 revenue growth; (2) a 
continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments, across rack-level and baseboard-based, 
riding on the growing demand in Neocloud and enterprises, (3) broadband, IoT, and 
game console entering peak season. We are positive on Pegatron’s expansion from 
consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive electronics, while the high memory 
costs could cap PC and smartphone market growth. Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Aug revenue was up 34% YoY / 3% MoM to NT$88bn, or 12% lower than our 
estimate. This reflects that high memory costs continue to cap PC and smartphone 
growth, despite a strong ramp-up in AI servers. Aug PC shipments was down 49% YoY to 
0.40-0.45m, reflecting the impact of consumption pull in under the high memory costs. 
Its major smartphone brand customer launched new models in Sep, supporting 
Pegatron’s growth in Sep and 4Q26; prior to September, the company suffered from 
model transition (i.e. declining demand for older models while new models were in a 
slow shipment ramp-up phase).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

### Page 3

NYPCB (8046.TW)
30 September 2026 Citi Research
3
BT substrate: improving industrywide supply with sustainable memory demand — In
the near term, while we do observe fading BT demand from smartphone, we also see
robust memory demand likely help offset the weakness in smartphone. T o note that,
NYPCB’s memory sales mix has increased to >60% currently from 30% in the past.
Although the market was disappointed about NYPCB’s muted BT price hike in 4Q26,

we think its BT demand, especially from memory products, is likely to be more resilient
than expected, with potential upsides in 4Q26E or 1Q27E. In the medium term, we also
observe Unimicron and SEMCO (report here) are converting its BT capacity into ABF
one, which would gradually benefit the industrywide BT supply side from 4Q26
onward. More meaningful outflowing BT orders would likely occur after 2Q27 with the
last BT orders placed by customers to Unimicron and SEMCO.

Demand continues to build up; upward earnings revisions; Buy — We see
favorable ABF/BT trend for NYPCB in the upcoming quarters. For its PCB segment
(with demand mostly from non-AI applications now), it seems to be stabilizing in
the past few quarters and likely sees some upsides from new applications. Thus,
we raise our 2026/27 /28E earnings by 2%/8%/6% to factor better demand and
price hikes on supply tightness. We raise our TP to NT$1,700 (30x 2027E EPS) from

NT$1,600 (30x 2027E EPS) and reiterate our Buy rating. We also open 90-day
upside CW for NYPCB, as we expect AI chip production ramp along with price hikes
would drive NYPCB upcoming monthly sales momentum in 4Q26E.

### Page 8

NYPCB (8046.TW)
30 September 2026 Citi Research
8
NYPCB
(8046.TW; NT$ 1340.0; 1; 30 Sep 26; 13:30)
Company description
Established in 1997, Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board (NYPCB) is a technology
subsidiary of the Formosa Plastics Group. Nan Ya PCB's main products
include conventional PCB, HDI, Flip Chip substrate (FCBGA and FCCSP), Wire
Bond Substrate, etc. The company has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
Kunshan, China.
Investment strategy
We rate NYPCB shares as Buy. We believe ABF/BT should see a solid recovery

in 2026 on robust AI demand: 1) NYPCB is the purest ABF play with a
significant earnings turnaround, driven by growing chip sizes and layer counts
for AI ASIC/networking; and 2) the BT business should benefit from price
hikes on rising memory demand and T glass shortage. Considering T glass
support from NYPCB’s parent company, we believe NYPCB would  have a
better chance to win additional AI ASIC/networking business going forward.
Valuation
Our DCF-based TP for NYPCB is NT$1,700. With a risk-free rate of 5.00% (10-

year government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7% and an equity beta
of 1.22 (YTD weekly share price), we calculate NYPCB's WACC as 13.4%. Our
TP is equivalent to 30x/13x 2027E EPS/BVPS, which reflects its high P/E
multiple during upcycles. NYPCB traded at 15-20x P/E during its 2003-2008
upcycle driven by PC/NB demand strength. In 2010-11 when the market was
recovering from the global financial crisis, NYPCB traded at 25-35x P/E. In the
last 2019-22 upcycle when ABF was in shortage, NYPCB traded at 30-40x

P/E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the NYPCB shares from reaching our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected ABF demand from key
customers; 2) BT business seasonality weakness and fewer-than-expected
price hikes; and 3) severe T glass shortages that cause a bottleneck to its
production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our views on Delta after c.4 months of share price 
weakness, driven by investor concerns on HVDC delays, and a slowdown 
in power consumption trend amid deceleration in Nvidia’s GPU shipment 
growth YoY. We remain positive on Delta over 2026-28E, as rising AI ASIC 
server trend should drive a meaningful increase in DC-DC power module 
ASPs (vs. Nvidia’s discrete form factor). We expect higher HVDC adoption 
from 2028E, driven by IT rack and power supply architecture changes.

What’s the impact: Component shortages are the key near-term 
bottleneck. We attribute the recent share-price weakness to slower-than-
expected revenue momentum in both 2Q26, due to vendor-sanction issue 
at Delta Thailand, and 3Q26E, due to a worsening shortage of power 
semiconductors. We believe the 2Q26 revenue shortfall was a one-off 
event, though 3Q26E growth is likely to remain constrained by the power-
semi shortage. While supply capacity should improve, the supply-demand 
gap will likely persist for some time. That said, end-demand should remain 
sound. Given the continued improvement in revenue momentum, we still 
expect operating margin to reach a record-high of 18.3% in 3Q26.

Long-term growth drivers. We see Delta as a key beneficiary of the AI 
ASIC server trend. We were previously concerned that rising AI ASIC 
adoption could hurt Delta, as ASICs have lower thermal design power 
(TDP) than Nvidia GPUs, but now believe that Delta will benefit from rising 
content value of DC-DC power modules, as hyperscalers are more willing 
to spend on DC-DC modules (vs. discrete) despite higher initial cost given 
their superior energy efficiency. We forecast DC-DC power module revenue 
to reach c.TWD60bn in 2026E (vs. TWD9bn in 2025 and our prior estimate 
of c.TWD20bn for 2026E). This figure could increase further with rising 
ASIC usage in 2027. We also believe HVDC adoption rate could reach at 
least 50% in 2028E (vs. our prior expectation of 30%), as power supply 
architecture is becoming increasingly bulky (to accommodate aluminium 
capacitors inside power supply amid the super capacitor shortage). Limited 
space in IT racks would force customers to adopt more power racks and 
likely equip them with high voltage versions to improve efficiency.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 2-15% on higher 
HVDC contribution. We lift our 12M TP to TWD3,088 (from TWD2,988) 
based on a lower target PER of 49x (from 55x) to reflect market sentiment, 
(at the high-end of its past-3-year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-
forward EPS. Key downside risk: lower-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2028E earnings is 21% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish assumption for HVDC adoption.

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

### Page 5

5

Delta Electronics (2308 TT): 30 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 30 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta's board has 7 non-independent and 5 independent directors with a term of three years.
The total number of directors and ratio of independent directors appear higher than most other
listed companies. Besides, according to Delta's CSR report, enhancing the board's functions is
one of the key goals for the company to achieve better corporate governance; also, the board
members' diversity and professionalism will be taken into consideration to ensure a good board
quality. Moreover, the company evaluates all the directors in the board using internal regulations
and an external independent institution each year and at least once every three years,
respectively. We believe Delta has implemented relatively more enhanced measure than most
listed firms to improve its board quality.
Capital management 2
During 2016-19, Delta's payout ratio was stable in the range of c.70%, but fell to 56% in 2020
and 53% in 2021. For 2020, it was mainly due to a sizeable non-cash re-valuation gain after the
acquisition of Delta Thailand (DET), while the company maintained more cash on hand to deal
with the uncertainties caused by COVID-19. However, the DPS growth from 2016 to 2021 was
limited, as Delta struggled with business diversification into new end-markets (eg, smartphone,
NEV). We believe that Delta's effort started to bear fruit in recent years after its NEV business
has gradually picked up. Overall, we see a stable dividend policy from Delta and expect its DPS
to continue to grow YoY in the future given that they declared a TWD11.6 DPS for 2025 which
equals a 50% payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue from related parties stood at 12% of total 2025 revenue and purchases from related
parties were at 11%. We see limited risk from related party transactions.
S Supply chain
management 1
Delta focuses on: 1) localised management, 2) supplier certification and risk management, and
3) supplier sustainability engagement as key initiatives under the scope of supply chain
management. In 2019, 144 new suppliers joined Delta's supply chain system. All of them passed
environmental and social evaluations, signed the declaration of "non-use of conflict materials",
and complied with Responsible Business Alliance's (BRA) Code of Conduct. Also, these vendors
had to implement and abide by Delta's supply chain CSR policy and code of conduct. To lower
ESG risks, Delta carries out RBA audits and monitors high-risk critical suppliers each year. The
suppliers that fail these audits have to provide detailed improvement plans in response to
identified issues. Besides, Delta has surveyed a total of 263 suppliers with potential risk and 63
suppliers with high risk of the total 1,331 suppliers, based on its CSR policy and code of conduct.
If these suppliers are flagged as high-risk and no concrete improvements are made, Delta may
consider terminating cooperation and transactions with them to improve the company's
engagement on supplier sustainability. Overall, we think Delta has a solid plan to improve its
supply chain management with specific KPIs, including: 1) achieving supplier improvement rate
of 80% in 2025, 2) reducing GHG emissions rate of critical suppliers by 10% in 2025, and 3)
improving water saving rate of critical suppliers by 12% in 2025.
S Data security 2
Delta's Information Security team created Delta's Information Security Policy in November 2013
and started providing educational training to global employees in 2014. Delta gradually
introduced the Information Security Management System (ISMS) in 2016 and obtained the ISO
27001 international certification in 2018. According to ISO27001, Delta has to conduct at least
one internal audit on information security and appoint an independent institution to conduct audit
externally. According to Delta, no major deficiencies have been found in recent years.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

NYPCB

Alarm cleared with new drivers into 2027; 
re-it. Buy with PO raise to NT$2,000

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,000 TWD | Price: 1,340 TWD

BT substrate price hike resumed from 4Q26 
Following (see report) the worries of moderating BT substrate price hike in early 
September, we now expect NYPCB to still raise relevant ASP by single-digit % in 4Q26, 
and the hike is purely driven by favorable S/D which will fully translate into the accretion 
in bottom-line. Following this, BT substrate price will likely be raised by another 20+% in 
1Q27, due partly to the cost pass-through. We currently bake in ~10% revenue QoQ with 
32-33% GM for 4Q26, but we do not rule out the possibility for its revenue to surprise 
on the upside, should there be a faster ramp of its ABF substrate capacity in Kunshan. 
New driver for 2027: server CPU & mix optimization  
Looking into 2027 we expect the benefits of demand overflow on (see report) server 
CPU side to materialize, and we flag a chance for the firm to record 20+% revenue from 
the emerging business with AMD server CPU, where NYPCB is working on the 
qualification in its Shulin site currently and should capture most of the spillover demand 
if the progress goes smooth. Meanwhile, the firm will likely allocate more capacity from 
other sites (e.g. Kunshan) to fulfill the demand from Broadcom, which also helps enhance 
the mix. Currently we model ~50% GM for its ABF substrate business in 2027, but we 
see chance for further upward revision, driven by more favorable S/D dynamics and mix.  
Raise estimates/PO; prime beneficiary of price hike 
NYPCB’s share price (up ~50%) lags its major peers Unimicron/Kinsus (up ~70%) since 
late-July, but in our view it is still the best proxy to position for price hike in the 
substrate market. Judging from (see report) Kinsus’ August prints last week, we believe 
NYPCB remains on track to meet or beat the market’s expectations on N-T margins, and 
we reiterate Buy on the stock. Along with 13%/18% EPS raise in 2027/28E thanks to the 
more benign pricing/margin outlook driven by further mix improvement and S/D, we 
raise PO to NT$2,000 (from NT$1,700) same based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1997, NYPCB was listed on the Taiwan Stock 
Exchange in 2006. The firm is a 67%-owned subsidiary of 
Nan Ya Plastics Corporation (1303 TT). NYPCB's main 
products are HDI, PCB and IC substrates (including ABF and 
BT substrates), widely used in telecommunication, 
consumers, PC, and Auto applications. The clients include 
AMD, Broadcom, Samsung, Apple, Continental, etc. The 
main competitors include Unimicron, Kinsus, Ibiden, 
Shinko, and SEMCO, etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on NYPCB, in view of its continuous 
improvement of utilization rate and gross margin across 
ABF and BT substrate business into 2027, thanks to the 
robust demand growth from fabless customers and 
demand overflow in AI ASICs.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
NYPCB (NANYF) 
Our PO of NT$2,000 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E. We view this multiple as fair 
given ROE and operating margin expansion to 39% and 41% in 2027-28E (vs 13% and 
14% in 2025-26E).

Upside risks to our PO are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) higher SiP/AiP adoption, and (3) 
less intensified competition pressure in PCB business.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) muted SiP/AiP adoption, 
and (3) more severe competition in PCB business.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price

### Page 1

Summary: After market hours on 5 October 2026, EMC, TUC, and ITEQ released their 
monthly sales results. Our read-through is positive as the three key CCL vendors beat 
sales expectations by 5-15% on better pricing and on-going spec upgrades. Our thesis is 
that the strong AI demand is causing a spill-over impact on the whole CCL industry with 
low-end CCL having c.100% price hike in 2026E and high-end CCL seeing a 40-50% price 
hike due to cost inflation in 2026E. Recent data points showed this thesis remains valid. 
Looking ahead, we expect high-end/low-end CCL to see additional c.10%/20% ASP 
increases in 4Q26E, which could be the key share price catalyst. We believe that recent 
negatives news (including the impact of PTFE and CPO/NPO on the industry) are 
overdone. The impact on high-end CCL from these new technologies is likely to be limited 
from the point of view of Taiwan CCL companies. As such, we remain positive that the 
three names in our CCL coverage will ride on the decent price hike trend given favourable 
supply-demand dynamics.

• EMC (2383 TT, TWD5,700, Buy [1]) 
EMC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD21.5bn (+7% MoM, +169% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD60.9bn; +29% QoQ, +142% YoY) reaching 112% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD54.4bn) and 110% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD55.5bn). We attribute 
the revenue strength to the higher-than-expected price hike and on-going spec upgrades 
(leading to higher ASPs). Management attributes c.50% of the MoM increase in revenue in 
September to the price hike and the other 50% came to product mix optimisation. Looking 
ahead, the company doesn’t rule out the potential to increase its ASP again in 4Q26 (after 
the first time in 2Q26) in order to reflect rising raw material prices since 2Q26. As such, we 
expect revenue to grow at least high teens again on the potential price head as well as 
ongoing spec upgrades.

EMC sees limited impact from PTFE and HC glass-fibre-free CCL. Based on our 
understanding, PTFE is still under certification accounting for only limited layers inside 
specified high-end PCB as of now. As such, its impact on the high-end CCL TAM should be 
limited. As for HC glass-fibre-free CCL, the rigidity of PCB using this type of CCL could be 
a key concern to us.

Lastly, regarding the recent Co-Packaged Optics/Near-Packaged Optics (CPO/NPO) 
news’s negative impact on the CCL TAM, we also believe it to be limited given the likely 
adoption date for CPO of 2030, based on EMC’s forecast. Besides, we believe the PCB 
board inside CPO scale-up switches at that time would still need to support an 800G/1.6T 
transmission speed (or even lower) which would still require high-end PCB transmission 
although the highest transmission bandwidth can increase to 3.2T via optics. Lastly, EMC 
believes the spec of CCL won’t downgrade to M4 in the worst-case scenario but would be 
at least M6. Last but not least, we believe this kind of CPO-connected AI server might only

Given its leading position in the CCL industry and the likely imbalance between supply and 
demand in 2027, we remain positive view on EMC. We have a Buy (1) rating on EMC, with 
a 12-month TP of TWD7,200, based on a target PER of 38x applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected AI server demand.

• TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,780, Buy [1]) 
TUC’s September 2026 revenue was TWD6.1bn (+4% MoM, +115% YoY), which led to its 
3Q26 sales (TWD17.9bn; +26% QoQ, +123% YoY) reaching 103% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD17.4bn) and 105% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD17.1bn). Looking 
ahead, we expect TUC to deliver at least QoQ growth in 4Q26 given 1) a price hike along 
with its peers for high-end CCL (eg. M8) and 2) some shipment delay from 3Q26 given 
flooding in Southeast Asia. For the longer term, the company is promising a continual spec 
upgrade trend in 2027 with new projects using M9-grade CCL (including low dk1, low dk2, 
and Q-glass version) in 2027 for high-end switches, AI servers, and optical transceivers. 
Considering the insufficient capacity in the CCL industry, we expect TUC to further optimise 
its product mix after acquiring more high-end CCL orders in future quarters (vs. a single 
major AI server client in 2026). We have a Buy (1) rating on TUC, with a 12-month TP of 
TWD2,320, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Key 
downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for switches/AI servers, and 2) market 
share loss to CCL peers.

• ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD751, Buy [1]) 
ITEQ’s September 2026 revenue was TWD5.8bn (+15% MoM, +111% YoY), which led to 
its 3Q26 sales (TWD15.6bn; +35% QoQ, +102% YoY) reaching 114% of our 3Q26 forecast 
(TWD13.7bn) and 108% of the Bloomberg consensus forecast (TWD14.5bn). The number 
for 3Q26 was higher than our estimate due likely to a stronger-than-expected price hike 
(c.30% in 3Q26 vs. expectation of 20%). Looking ahead to 4Q26, we expect the company 
to further raise its pricing by 20-30% to factor in cost inflation in raw materials (E-glass) 
and the supply-demand gap. The company also expects the rising supply of e-glass (which 
could lift its utilisation rate from the current level of 75%) to lift its gross margin more 
significantly. For 2027, the company is excited about the new CPU adoption driving rising 
M7 CCL usage and is working on M9 certification with new end customers, which could 
become a key growth driver in 2028. We have a Buy (1) rating on ITEQ. Key downside 
risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

Document 085: 261005_ml_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261005_ml_TSMC.pdf

Date: 2026-10-05

### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

TSMC-Musk: customer relationship intact; 
tech/control sharing with Terafab unlikely

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,500 TWD

Customer neutrality central to TSMC’s foundry model 
Elon Musk on Saturday October 3rd responded “Just discussions, but something may come 
of it” to an article discussing the potential manufacturing partnership between TSMC 
and Terafab. From chipset design and foundry service perspective, we expect a deeper 
collaboration as Tesla and SpaceX grow at TSMC (2-3% of its sales) directly with their 
auto/robotics and cloud AI ASICs and indirectly through merchant HPC chipset buys (e.g. 
GPUs, networking switches) based on our estimate. However, the likelihood of TSMC 
licensing technology or operating a fab with Terafab holding a meaningful share should 
be low. Specifically, we would note TSMC’s JV fab record is limited including WaferTech, 
SSMC, VIS, JASM, ESMC and AVSM mostly on mature nodes. The company did not co-
own a fab with Apple who contributed ~21% of its revenue from ’15-25 mostly on the 
advanced processes as it keeps full business control and preserves customer neutrality. 
Terafab capex insufficient for 1 terawatt capacity target 
Based on Terafab’s applications under Texas’s JETI program, it outlined ~US$119bn of 
planned investment across 4 phases from ’26-36, with ~60% of it on tool procurement. 
The spending in ’26-28 would be highly skewed toward facility construction before it 
starts to pull in US$6-8bn equipment per annum from ‘29-36. Based on our estimate of 
US$600mn+ for every 1k WPM expansion on the advanced nodes in the US, Terafab 
could only grow its capacity installed base by ~10k WPM per annum at maximum even if 
it can successfully deliver the technology roadmap through its own R&D effort and Intel 
partnership. This would prevent Terafab’s operation from the economies of scale as the 
node migration continues, keeping TSMC as the major foundry partner for Tesla/SpaceX. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We keep our Buy rating on TSMC with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E (vs. current 16x).

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

### Page 3

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
06 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,500 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue is now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and should be well positioned to capitalize on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or
production bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing
metrology shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks
(50-60% of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

M
Update
Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
September Sales Flat MoM/
+30% YoY
In this report, we focus on Hiwin's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• September sales were NT$2,720mn (flat MoM/+30% YoY).
• 3Q26 revenue came in at NT$8,110mn (+8% QoQ/+35% YoY), largely in line
with our estimate at NT$7,996mn (+6% QoQ/+33% YoY) and the  consensus
of NT$7,964mn (+6% QoQ/+33% YoY).
Our view:
• On top of the sequential growth on the top line, we believe that Hiwin

should also further expand its gross margin on a QoQ basis in 3Q26, owing
to the higher utilization and the tailwinds of price hikes.
• We think the momentum should remain favorable in the near term.
• We stay constructive on the stock, with current valuation at 29x 2027e P/E
vs. a peak cycle level of 35-40x.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.
